
# Research Plan

## Experiment Scope

Title: Forensic Replication of the Paper

This Experiment reconstructs the paper’s GARD simulation, compositional preprocessing, self-replicator labels, \(\Phi^r\) analysis, prediction task, and intervention experiment. Because the authors’ exact code is unavailable, the outcome is a forensic replication with an explicit ambiguity ledger.

## Objective

Determine which principal reported results reproduce under defensible reconstructions and which depend on unstated implementation decisions.

## Parameters

- **Experiment ID:** E01
- **Experiment number:** 1
- **Group-plan relationship:** Foundational Experiment that creates the engines, frozen specifications, trajectories, and evidence base for all downstream work.
- **Baseline regime:** \(N_g=100\), \(n_{\min}=40\), \(n_{\max}=80\), \(A=-4\), \(\sigma=4\), 100 generations, `max_steps=1000`, and 100 independent catalytic matrices.
- **MRR:** 12 baseline runs and six paired max/control/min intervention triplets.
- **Scale-up:** 100 baseline runs and 100 paired triplets for the primary specification.
- **Compute ceiling:** 250 CPU-hours and 80 GPU-hours.

## Where We Stand

### Current Evidence

- **Evidence:** S01 completed a schema-valid ledger of 59 independent paper targets and 12 source discrepancies. S02 enumerated 105 unique ambiguity parameters and produced a non-executable 120-parameter registry with no silent defaults. S03 recovered the equation-bearing official arXiv v1 PDF, pinned historical GARD, modern GARD, `phyid`, and optional OmegaID to commits and trees, froze 39 dependency wheels and a 38-package hash lock, recorded CPU/GPU/precision identities, and passed an isolated Python 3.13 CPU/GPU smoke test. S04 implemented the pinned historical catalytic-matrix transform, join/leave propensities, explicit-draw event loop, fixed-size fission, first-child lineage continuation, and both non-drift techniques. S05 independently implemented complete typed specifications, integer states, explicit propensities, categorical/Gillespie/vector-Poisson kernels, separated modern RNG inputs, configurable growth/fission/daughter semantics, and complete diagnostic logs. S05 passed 22/22 unit/independence/provenance checks and 8/8 predeclared matched-branch distributional gates; across 512 propensity cases the maximum absolute error versus S04 was zero and event, even-fission, odd-fission, and one-generation endpoint total-variation distances were 0.00216, 0.01274, 0.01413, and 0.00770. S06 froze nine immutable, domain-separated PCG64DXSM stream identities (including the S05-required waiting-time stream), checksum-protected canonical JSON, and a lossless trajectory schema. Its 14-event/3-fission example and all three explicit S05 branch profiles passed Draft 2020-12 conformance, invariant checks, byte-exact round trips, and exact same-engine regeneration under an identity-recorded one-thread numeric policy. S07 preregistered a 26-test global family at family-wise alpha 0.005, retained rare categories in exact unpooled Monte Carlo tests, and passed 26/26 stochastic tests, 54/54 deterministic invariants, and 7/7 failure injections. S08 froze source-traceable historical `H>0.9` technique 1 plus explicit validation-only cosine, Euclidean, and strict-positive Aitchison threshold-graph configurations, three past-only companions, 44 total materialized configuration IDs, and 170 sensitivity evaluations before outcome inspection. All 40 S08 validation checks passed across five validated fixtures (43 observations): 301/301 canonical output rows were present, 172/172 four-family reference rows were complete, exact label-array regeneration passed, and every null label had a reason. Fixture-level family disagreement was substantive: pooled positive-label Jaccards were 0.571–0.829 and pooled binary adjusted Rand indices were 0.062–0.438 across nonidentical families; retrospective-versus-past-only comparisons flipped 21 labels, always retrospective-positive/past-only-negative. S09 then froze and evaluated 13 zero treatments (six additive, six matched-delta multiplicative, and one no-replacement control), every dimension-2 and dimension-4 dropped-component CLR, Helmert ILR, full CLR, raw, Hellinger, and data-scoped principal-log-ratio controls. Across all 43 observations it emitted 4,901/4,901 status-bearing transform rows: 4,745 eligible finite outputs and 156 explicit ineligible outputs. The maximum inverse error was 2.00e-15; all 178 evaluable full-CLR/ILR/PLR isometry checks and 8/8 failure injections passed; exact replay reproduced four anchor artifacts byte-for-byte. Of 247 fixture-level complete specifications, 246 were accepted within explicit eligibility domains and one no-replacement PLR fixture specification was not evaluable because only one strictly positive row existed.
- **S10 evidence:** S10 froze its benchmark contract at Git commit `02d38634f47e73d95882846aeeb89820f38a98b0` before inspecting outcomes, then evaluated 448 source-reference cases, 2,176 theory comparisons, 368 invariance/shuffle controls, 92 backend comparisons, 864 partition-search results, 1,656 candidate partition scores, and 232 unique S06 estimator-stream identities. The pinned phyid reference passed independent-Gaussian, exact redundant-copy, exact XOR, noisy-redundant Gaussian, directional VAR, time-shuffle, affine/relabel, lattice-closure, and MATLAB-fixture gates. Maximum ensemble atom error was 0.00522 nats across the preregistered theory comparisons; lattice closure was 2.22e-16. OmegaID CPU agreed with phyid in all 44 comparisons (maximum local absolute error 3.11e-13), and OmegaID GPU agreed with CPU in all 44 (maximum 2.11e-13). All three primary exhaustive planted-partition objectives recovered `[0,1]|[2,3]` in 8/8 replicates. The overall outcome is nevertheless constraining/contradictory because both OmegaID CPU and GPU failed all 16/16 independent binary-relabel controls per backend, while phyid passed 64/64 discrete relabel controls and every Gaussian backend passed every affine control.
- **S11 evidence:** S11 froze a distinct four-fold OAS-cross-fit Gaussian PhiID branch and stability-spectral/single-flip partition branch at Git commit `f257146cd845f49ec01efe08175a15cae64ccf39` before inspecting outcomes. It validated all 16 exact fixed pairs at 24–255 effective samples and D=99/100, retained MMI and experimental CCS separately, and left S10's strict >=512 gate unchanged. The result is constraining/contradictory: 11/16 gate families passed, including 32/32 held-out null calibrations, 8/8 CCS population-oracle checks, 32/32 regularization rows, 190/190 CPU/GPU comparisons, 956/956 causality rows, 12/12 injections, exact reproducibility, registry preservation, and output suppression. Known truth passed 46/64 summaries, shuffle passed 28/64 rows, invariance passed 356/576 attempts, and all 48 partition summary gates failed; dimension-8 approximate/exhaustive agreement was 0.326–0.850 versus the frozen 0.90 gate. - **Known caveats:** Registry v0.3.0 remains byte-for-byte unchanged and intentionally non-executable: 64 items are unresolved, conflicting, or evidence-deferred and 21 frozen branch sets require separate immutable specification IDs. S04 found that public historical v10 uses single weighted events, accumulates rate sums rather than Gillespie waiting time, has no `max_steps` condition, samples fixed-size daughters without replacement, follows child A, and initializes with replacement under legacy global RNG ordering. S05/S07 agreement is distributional and confined to explicitly matched public-historical validation profiles; it neither identifies the unavailable author implementation nor implies exact MATLAB/cross-RNG trajectories. S06 is a reconstruction-engineering contract, not evidence of author or legacy MATLAB RNG semantics. Exact regeneration is limited to the frozen independent engine, adapter, NumPy/runtime/platform, and precision identities; cross-platform floating-point agreement is bounded for audit but full-trajectory equality is not guaranteed. S07 small-system non-rejection does not prove implementation identity or validate scientific claims. S08 label overlaps are fixture diagnostics, not baseline prevalence or paper-claim results; the Euclidean 0.1 and Aitchison 0.5 anchors, graph linkage, minimum size 3, medoid tie rule, and ineligible policy are versioned validation branches rather than recovered author defaults. Historical labels are not author-code identity, retrospective branches use future observations, and optional historical technique 2 preserved 8/15 source-domain errors without repair. S09 did not recover an author zero policy, feature layout, invalid-row policy, or paper-default delta. Full CLR and raw closed proportions remain structurally covariance-singular; dropped CLR is reversible but not isometric, and its distance correlation with ILR varied by dropped component. Multiplicative replacement legitimately leaves all-zero states ineligible, while additive pseudocounts map them to a declared uniform composition. S09 fixture/pool conditioning does not establish full-trajectory estimator readiness, and frozen S08 labels were not retroactively changed. Historical GARD has no detected license, the parent OCI digest remains unavailable, and two L4 GPUs were visible despite the generic one-GPU description.
- **S10 caveats:** S10 validates named reconstruction branches, not the unavailable author implementation, MATLAB RNG, author PhiID atom mapping, redundancy, estimator, MIB objective, normalization, search, or partition-to-series mapping. The paper equation is preserved as the derived aggregate `str+stx+sty+sts-rtr-rtx-rty-rts`, not relabeled as a source-named atom. Pinned phyid CCS remains experimental because its source docstrings say “To be implemented.” OmegaID's discrete 2×2 path uses a global matrix mean for binarization and is ineligible after scale/relabel failure; its more-than-2×2 doublet path is an ineligible substitute for the 16-atom lattice. OmegaID vector parts require equal widths and therefore cannot encode arbitrary 99-component bipartitions. Exhaustive MIB evidence is only four-dimensional; PC1-plus-spectral failed 3/72 comparisons, and all partition branches remain validation-only rather than paper-primary. The six-effective-sample MATLAB source fixture is regression-only and fails the S10 science-sample gate by design.
- **S11 caveats:** S11 is validation-only and used synthetic fixtures because S12 trajectories did not yet exist at its execution time. The new fixed-window estimator failed mandatory known-truth, structured-shuffle, partition-search, and relabel-invariance gates; good null calibration, regularization, closure, or GPU agreement cannot override those failures. The independent-white null envelope was not exchangeable with shuffled systems retaining strong contemporaneous redundancy. D=99 consensus partitions were typically one component from the planted split, D=8 approximation did not meet exhaustive agreement, and spectral partitioning was not feature-relabel invariant. Strict expanding results are conditional feasibility evidence and whole-trajectory results are non-prospective. No fixed numerical output, author method, paper-primary branch, Omega discrete result, or Omega doublet substitute is eligible.
- **S11R caveats:** S11R is also synthetic validation only and is not author-code, MATLAB-RNG, or paper-primary recovery. The condition-matched null and permutation-equivariant D=99/100 partition repairs succeeded, but simultaneous validation still failed because three directional-VAR conditions per redundancy reversed the required directional ordering and every D=8 pair missed exhaustive agreement; 3,396/18,432 D=8 comparisons were explicitly ineligible due frozen objective-tie handling. The Wishart correction theorem is Gaussian and does not establish calibration for serially overlapping or non-Gaussian GARD data. CCS remains experimental. The bounded repair path is finished and may not be continued without new authorization.
- **S12 caveats:** S12 is a materially changed strict expanding-window scope, not author-code, fixed-window, early-time, Figure 6, or Table 1 recovery. Its broad late-time numerical coverage does not validate localized spikes. Ten pinned-source whole branches failed closed, 14/36 actual-trajectory numerical checkpoints missed the joint `1e-10` policy, and 99.9083% of authorized treated action opportunities were nonseparable. The single applied action cannot support an intervention effect. MMI and experimental CCS remain distinct validation identities, OmegaID Gaussian was cross-check-only, and no silent regularization or substitute was used.
- **S12B caveats:** S12B is a source-informed forensic reconstruction, not the unpublished GARD implementation. The mandatory singular-status mismatch blocks scientific use of both wrappers under the frozen global gate and does not show how either source family behaves on the twelve GARD trajectories. No post-outcome repair, scope reduction, retrospective result, prefix result, association, spike audit, or suffix-invariance test was attempted after the stop. S10–S12 artifacts and source snapshots remained byte-exact.
- **S12C caveats:** S12C is still source-informed rather than author- or paper-primary. Punctuated completed-fit traces were observed, but neither implementation met the preregistered positive coherence rule. Completed-fit estimates use future observations and are descriptive only; their material disagreement with prefix refits is evidence of future dependence, not prospective early warning. A single trajectory correlation per prefix estimand was undefined because its label lacked within-run variation and was retained as undefined. Nonfinite local values were status-bearing and below frozen stop thresholds. `SOURCE_FAMILY_NOT_SUPPORTED` applies to the twelve frozen baselines and cannot prove that every possible GARD implementation lacks a signal. No further equivalence repair, intervention, S13, or substitution for S12 is authorized.
- **S12D caveats:** S12D confirms source-metric identity on fixtures but supplies no scientific association evidence. The unchanged historical GARD branch can produce extinction before a requested fission count; C02’s terminal loss event changed mass from 1 to 0 during generation 5. Analyzing only the 23 survivors, replacing C02, changing the fixed generation identifiers, or adding an extinction/missingness rule would be a post-lock scope change and was not done. The cache-only partial trajectories are represented by hashes and failure status, not promoted as a valid confirmation dataset. No result changes S12C or authorizes S13.
- **S12E caveats:** S12E tested only the five frozen, source-grounded engine candidates and does not prove that every possible unpublished implementation has the wrong time base. The paper-inferred target of 813.6 steps is derived from 716/0.88, while the hard compatibility interval was preregistered at 500–1,500. K1–K3 otherwise completed cleanly and had plausible post-fission masses, but their median time scale failed the frozen gate; no threshold was relaxed after inspection. Consequently self-replication, information-theory, future-dependence, and intervention questions are `NOT_REACHED`, not negative results.
- **S12F caveats:** The terminal failure is an operational replay-comparator failure, not evidence that fixed or adaptive exposure cannot match the paper. The failing pair identity and numeric distance were not retained or diagnosed after the mandated stop. Static inspection identifies NaN-bearing zero-update generation summaries as a possible comparator risk, but S12F did not rerun or repair it. Benchmark h=0.25 trajectories spanned 1,083–1,358 C0 steps on one shared matrix depending on daughter/overshoot semantics, which is feasibility context only—not an ABC posterior or confirmed timebase candidate.

## Method Sketch

- **Approach:** Implement two GARD engines, validate them on small systems, reconstruct labels and \(\Phi^r\), pass an MRR, and then reproduce descriptive, predictive, and intervention findings.
- **Controls:** Independent engine implementation, hand-calculated states, shuffled controls, known information-dynamic systems, CPU/GPU agreement, common-random-number intervention comparisons, and block-aware statistics.
- **Failure modes:** Hidden historical-code semantics, zero-induced infinities, singular covariance, unstable MIBs, temporal leakage, seed drift, and intervention score mismatch.
- **Reproducibility notes:** Pin every source commit; preserve raw events or exact regeneration instructions; freeze seed families; hash artifacts; label non-prospective analyses.

## Research Step Queue

### S01: Create the E01 claim ledger

#### Frozen question or hypothesis

The paper’s claims can be decomposed into independently testable targets.

#### Full description

Record the aggregate trend, spike, correlation, group comparison, prediction, spike-timing, and intervention claims separately. Include exact reported values and source locations.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome supportive.
- **Method family:** Forensic claim extraction.
- **Expected outputs:** Claim ledger CSV and Markdown; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S01/research_step_full_results.md`.
- **Output directory:** `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S01/`
- **Validation checks:** Passed second-pass extraction, source reconciliation, schema validation, three focused tests, Ruff, and artifact hash checks.
- **Completion criteria:** Met: 59/59 claims have a unique ID, source, statistic, direction, unit, estimand, and pass rule.
- **Caveats or blockers:** Twelve source discrepancies were preserved; S02 has now mapped every one, but downstream evidence is still required before claim adjudication.
- **Planned next action:** Superseded by completed S02.
- **Current next action:** None for S01; its prior recommendations are superseded by completed S02–S05.

### S03: Freeze source and environment snapshots

#### Frozen question or hypothesis

The reconstruction can be made immutable and rerunnable despite external repository changes.

#### Full description

Pin the historical GARD repositories, modern GARD repository, `phyid`, NumPy, SciPy, scikit-learn, PyTorch, Numba, and optional ΩID source. Capture runtime image or container digest and support CPU-only and GPU-assisted paths without silent precision changes.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome supportive.
- **Method family:** Reproducible environment engineering.
- **Expected outputs:** Source manifest, lock files, environment report, license notes, source archives or commit references; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S03/research_step_full_results.md`.
- **Output directory:** `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S03/`
- **Validation checks:** Passed immutable commit/tree/ref/file/archive verification; hash-locked dependency resolution; wheel/hash/license inventory; clean isolated Python 3.13 CPU/GPU smoke; precision audit; source-only registry delta and sentinel-preservation audit; Ruff; 13/13 repository tests; S01/S02 output-integrity check; and S04 absence check.
- **Completion criteria:** Met with explicit boundary: all required source and dependency identities are frozen, source archives or immutable references are recorded, and a rerunnable isolated environment passed. The unavailable parent OCI digest is represented by a sentinel plus a composite runtime fingerprint rather than a guessed digest.
- **Caveats or blockers:** No source patch was required for Python 3.13; `phyid` emitted a missing-README packaging warning only. Author code, exact Phi-r-to-`phyid` mapping, and redundancy choice remain unresolved. Historical GARD and modern GARD root license gaps prohibit assuming redistribution permission. Two L4s were visible, so later runs must choose an explicit UUID.
- **Planned next action:** Superseded by completed S04.
- **Current next action:** None for S03; its prior S04 recommendation is superseded by completed S04 and S05.

### S04: Construct a historical-reference GARD engine

#### Frozen question or hypothesis

The public historical equations and control semantics can be translated without changing their stochastic behavior.

#### Full description

Port or wrap event rates, the lognormal catalytic matrix, stochastic growth, fission, daughter selection, and historical non-drift analysis. Retain a compatibility mode for historical conventions.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome supportive.
- **Method family:** Forensic software reconstruction.
- **Expected outputs:** Reference engine, tests, compatibility notes, small fixtures; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S04/research_step_full_results.md`.
- **Output directory:** `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S04/`
- **Validation checks:** Passed hand-calculated catalytic transform and propensities, strict weighted-event boundaries, exact ±1 mass changes, rate-sum accumulation, split-size/extinction behavior, even/odd fission, first-child continuation, two-generation lineage, historical H/non-drift alignment, source edge-failure preservation, nine file hashes, 14 source-line mappings, 19/19 registry mappings, 11 API no-default checks, Ruff, and 24/24 repository tests.
- **Completion criteria:** Met: the Git-backed engine reproduces 15 verified small historical cases under explicit draw tapes and publishes compatibility/source-traceability artifacts without claiming author-code identity.
- **Caveats or blockers:** Exact legacy MATLAB RNG streams remain unresolved; deterministic draw tapes bypass rather than resolve that issue. No MATLAB/Octave runtime was present. Historical fission, initialization, update/time, and absent `max_steps` behavior conflict with or underdetermine paper prose; the no-license boundary prohibits source redistribution assumptions.
- **Planned next action:** Build an independent engine only if separately authorized.
- **Current next action:** None for S04; its prior S05 recommendation is superseded by completed S05.

A separately written implementation can reproduce the same model-level distributions.

#### Full description

Implement integer states, explicit propensity arrays, modern random generators, growth limits, fission, and complete event logging without copying historical control flow.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome supportive.
- **Method family:** Independent stochastic simulation.
- **Expected outputs:** Independent engine, API, tests, benchmarks; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S05/research_step_full_results.md`.

- **Completion criteria:** Met: no unexplained model-level disagreement occurred on the matched small benchmarks; maximum propensity error was zero and event/fission/one-generation distances were within frozen tolerances.

Separate seed streams prevent accidental coupling and permit exact regeneration.

Separate catalytic-matrix, initial-state, event, fission, intervention, estimator, and machine-learning seeds. Save pre-event state, event, total propensity, post-event state, generation, fission result, and daughter choice.

- **Completion criteria:** Met: the same versioned specification and nine-stream manifest regenerated an identical 14-event/3-fission trajectory payload, checksum, and canonical bytes under the frozen independent-engine identity.
- **Caveats or blockers:** The canonical PCG64DXSM hierarchy is reconstruction engineering, not an author/MATLAB RNG inference. Public historical GARD remains explicit-draw-tape-only and nonconforming to canonical seeded replay. Exact regeneration is scoped to commit/source/schema/precision/Python/NumPy/platform/runtime identity. Cross-platform audit requires absolute and relative error at most (10^{-12}) and at most 8 ULP for corresponding finite floats, but any changed discrete event, state, fission, daughter, or stopping reason is failure. Registry v0.3.0 remains unchanged, non-executable, and sentinel-bearing.
- **Planned next action:** Superseded by completed S07.
- **Current next action:** None for S06; its prior S07 recommendation is superseded by completed S07.

### S07: Validate stochastic behavior before scientific analysis

#### Frozen question or hypothesis

The simulators generate the intended event and catalytic-matrix distributions.

#### Full description

Use fixed small systems to compare sampled event frequencies with \(a_k/a_0\), test empirical \(\log\beta\) mean and variance against \(A\) and \(\sigma^2\), verify \(\pm1\) event mass changes, and validate fission distributions.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome supportive. Its handoff correctly left S08 absent; S08 is now complete.
- **Method family:** Statistical simulation validation.
- **Expected outputs:** Goodness-of-fit tables, diagnostic plots, validation fixtures; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S07/research_step_full_results.md`.
- **Output directory:** `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S07/`

- **Completion criteria:** Met: both engines passed all preregistered stochastic and invariant tolerances where configured to the same explicit branch, and independent-only branches passed their separate analytical targets.
- **Caveats or blockers:** Historical NumPy draws do not identify legacy MATLAB RNG, and neither engine is author code. The paper vector-Poisson exposure/clipping/boundary profile remains an explicit reconstruction rather than a default. Exact Monte Carlo p-values have finite resolution; non-rejection on small fixtures does not prove identity. Registry v0.3.0 remains unchanged, non-executable, and sentinel-bearing. The first canonical invocation failed before writing outcomes due to an interface accessor error; the unchanged preregistration was rerun after a tested, committed correction, with the attempt preserved in provenance.

- **Current next action:** None for S07; its prior recommendation is superseded by completed S08.

### S09: Resolve compositional zeros transparently

#### Frozen question or hypothesis

Valid compositional transforms can be applied without infinities, singular failures, or hidden row deletion.

#### Full description

Run the pseudocount grid, multiplicative replacement, dropped CLR, and ILR. Record zero frequency, transform conditioning, covariance rank, and representation agreement.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome supportive. Its S10 recommendation is superseded by completed S10.
- **Method family:** Compositional preprocessing.
- **Expected outputs:** Transform library, diagnostics, valid-specification registry; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S09/research_step_full_results.md`.
- **Output directory:** `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S09/`
- **Validation checks:** Passed 28/28 frozen-input checks plus 17/17 preregistered provenance, complete-row, zero-grid, dropped-component, finite-value, explicit-ineligibility, inverse, basis, isometry, schema/checksum, failure-injection, zero-reconciliation, diagnostics, figure, and S10-absence checks; 4,901/4,901 lossless rows; 178/178 evaluable isometries; 8/8 injections; byte-exact replay of four anchor artifacts; 8 focused and 82 non-identity-pinned repository tests; Ruff; visual inspection; pushed commits.
- **Completion criteria:** Met. All 246 accepted complete specifications have explicit zero/eligibility semantics and passed finite/inverse validation; one no-replacement PLR fixture specification is explicitly not evaluable rather than defaulted or dropped.
- **Caveats or blockers:** CLR remains linearly dependent before component removal, and raw closed proportions have the same covariance-rank defect. Multiplicative replacement leaves all-zero states ineligible. Dropped CLR is invertible but non-isometric. PLR is fit-scope dependent. No author zero policy, delta, feature layout, invalid policy, or paper primary was recovered; the registry remains unchanged and non-executable.
- **Planned next action:** Validate \(\Phi\) implementation only after separate authorization.
- **Current next action:** None; the prior S10 recommendation is superseded by completed S10.

### S10: Validate the information-dynamic implementation

#### Frozen question or hypothesis

The selected implementation has expected behavior on systems with known information structure.

#### Full description

Evaluate independent Gaussian processes, redundant copies, XOR-like synergy, coupled autoregressive systems, time shuffles, and scale transformations. Cross-check reference and optional accelerated implementations.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome constraining/contradictory. S11 subsequently completed and preserved the strict S10 boundary.
- **Method family:** Estimator validation.
- **Expected outputs:** Synthetic benchmark suite, estimator comparison tables, tolerance policy; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S10/research_step_full_results.md`.
- **Output directory:** `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S10/`
- **Validation checks:** Frozen preregistration commit and 28 frozen-input identities; 448 reference cases; 2,176 theory comparisons; 368 invariant/shuffle controls; 92 source/backend comparisons; 864 MIB-search results; 1,656 candidate scores; 232/232 unique seed identities; 10/10 failure injections; 10/10 focused and 92/92 non-state-sensitive E01 tests; Ruff; artifact/hash and visual checks.
- **Completion criteria:** Partially met in a scientifically constraining way. The reference passed every benchmark and Gaussian accelerated paths agreed within declared precision, but OmegaID discrete CPU/GPU failed every independent relabel-invariance control and are ineligible.
- **Caveats or blockers:** No author mapping/default was recovered. OmegaID discrete uses global matrix-mean binarization; Omega doublet is not a 16-atom substitute; equal-width vectors cannot cover arbitrary 99-component partitions; exhaustive MIB validation is small-dimensional; PC1 spectral failed 3/72 approximation comparisons; CCS source status is experimental; finite-sample and strict covariance gates remain mandatory. The strict gate requires at least 512 effective samples, so 0/16 queued fixed-window/lag pairs are eligible.
- **Planned next action:** The original plan proposed time-localized \(\Phi^r\); S10 constrains that proposal to expanding/whole scopes with at least 512 effective samples unless a new small-window branch is preregistered and validated.
- **Current next action:** Superseded by completed S11; none.

#### Parameters

- **Status:** Completed on 2026-08-01; outcome constraining/contradictory; returned `RETURN_FOR_REVIEW_VALIDATION_BLOCKED`. S12 was later separately authorized and completed without changing S11.
- **Method family:** Sliding-window information dynamics.
- **Expected outputs:** \(\Phi^r\) arrays, partition histories, runtime benchmarks, specification metadata; `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S11/research_step_full_results.md`.
- **Output directory:** `$ARTIFACTS_DIR/research_steps/S11/`

#### S19-L19–L42: Autonomous Organization-Before-Onset Discovery and Confirmation Program

- **Human authorization:** Continue beyond L18 through at most L42 without an intermediate Chief handoff; stop earlier if a scientifically valid solution is found. If no solution is found by L42, stop for human review.
- **Program objective:** Identify a reproducible signal of organization, measurable strictly before first entry into a non-saturated recurring-attractor state, that adds information beyond elapsed time, adjacent similarity/exact H, ordinary stability, and prefix recurrence geometry.
- **Sequential boundary:** Exactly one bounded loop is active at a time. Every loop receives a pre-outcome method lock, separate artifacts, full negative-result retention, numerical/replay/suffix-integrity checks, and a chronological ledger entry. A null prunes a method family; it does not authorize within-loop outcome-guided tuning.
- **Discovery/confirmation firewall:** S13Y/L18 and other previously examined cohorts may support discovery only. A solution requires at least one later untouched, seed-firewalled cohort with the complete target, landmark/horizon, model, features, controls, thresholds, and success gates frozen before simulation. Untouched confirmation units may not be replaced after outcomes.
- **Minimum success conditions:** Adequate at-risk/event/non-event support; exact suffix invariance of all prospective inputs; candidate-2 and candidate-3 directional agreement; matrix-level uncertainty; multiplicity-aware improvement over time, exact-H/stability, recurrence geometry, and a complete ordinary-dynamics baseline; calibration and label/feature/time permutation controls; no completed-run centroid or other future-dependent input; and exact replay/regeneration. A result that exists only on the discovery cohort, one candidate, completed-fit information, or a proxy control is not a solution.
- **Initial staged themes:** L19 source-grounded early-warning observables; later loops may test recurrence quantification, multiscale geometry/topology, local operator/Koopman dynamics, landmark survival formulations, outcome-blind representations, and compact cross-candidate reaction coordinates. Themes activate only when nonduplicative and information-adding. Confirmation begins as soon as a sufficiently precise lead exists; unused loop numbers need not be consumed.
- **Hard exclusions:** No favorable-candidate pooling, molecular-row pseudoreplication, concealed failed specification, author contact, intervention work, S20, E02, or report-bundle generation. The immutable S18 paper-facing verdict is not changed by this separate exploratory program.
- **Current active loop:** None. The autonomous program continued additively through untouched L54 confirmation and stopped successfully; all unused later loop authorizations expired at E01 closeout.

## Gate

- **PASS-STRONG:** Association, prospective prediction, and bidirectional intervention effects reproduce with block-aware or paired 95% confidence intervals excluding zero.
- **PASS-PARTIAL:** Two of the three reproduce robustly.
- **HOLD:** At least one central result depends on an unresolved undocumented choice.
- **FAIL:** GARD replication occurs but none of the causal-architecture claims survives.
